DeineFraesmaschine

134

DeineFraesmaschine

Identification lead

Main wiring diagram · SmoothieBox

Relay ratings below are corrected from Panasonic catalog pages 81–82; the main drawing now includes the current canonical source graph; fitted hardware remains unqualified.

Why this controller: Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate.

Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior STEP/DIR/ENABLE contacts feed one depicted SN74LVC07A interface per DQ860 axis. Retain the external motor power stages and qualify the spindle analog/run interface and limit inputs separately.

Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate. Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior case contacts with retained external power stages. The main diagram and connection-ID schedule define the current proposed routes. Dotted GUESS routes need the named commissioning checks; OPEN routes remain disconnected until the missing terminal, rating or interface is established. Previous Core alternatives remain only in the superseded historical architecture record.

Current installation sequence
  1. Match the actual machine and controller revisions, trace the unpowered harness and verify all signal/reference, power and independent safety boundaries before energising. Follow the current connection IDs rather than the superseded architecture prose.
  2. Controller logic supply · separate from motor power: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Verify exact board supply path and rail ratings; motor current uses separate wiring and fuses.
  3. X external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  4. Y external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  5. Z external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  6. A external driver · STEP/DIR/ENABLE and all three field grounds: One SN74LVC07A per axis, three loaded open-drain sinks; at 16mA each total48mA rather than six loaded sinks near100mA absolute package limit. Verify guaranteed VOL, input thresholds, current and thermal conditions against the fitted drive. New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.
  7. DQ860 driver A · X motor power and four separate winding leads: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DQ860 driver A is an individually drawn circuit. The Rev A source plan shows four separate driver blocks but does not map block A to axis X; this pairing is a labelled guess. Connect the two power conductors and four individual phase outputs only after verifying the fitted driver and tracing the motor leads.
  8. DQ860 driver B · Y motor power and four separate winding leads: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DQ860 driver B is an individually drawn circuit. The Rev A source plan shows four separate driver blocks but does not map block B to axis Y; this pairing is a labelled guess. Connect the two power conductors and four individual phase outputs only after verifying the fitted driver and tracing the motor leads.
  9. DQ860 driver C · Z motor power and four separate winding leads: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DQ860 driver C is an individually drawn circuit. The Rev A source plan shows four separate driver blocks but does not map block C to axis Z; this pairing is a labelled guess. Connect the two power conductors and four individual phase outputs only after verifying the fitted driver and tracing the motor leads.
  10. DQ860 driver D · A motor power and four separate winding leads: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DQ860 driver D is an individually drawn circuit. The Rev A source plan shows four separate driver blocks but does not map block D to axis A; this pairing is a labelled guess. Connect the two power conductors and four individual phase outputs only after verifying the fitted driver and tracing the motor leads.
  11. Hitachi WJ200 · SmoothieBox PWM to O-L analog speed reference: Use the actual contact labels and per-wire qualification in the current main diagram. Any older Core/header reference in retained source context is not the selected controller endpoint.
  12. Hitachi WJ200 · separate configured forward-run input and line/motor boundary: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. The WJ200 terminal map includes P24/PLC and inputs 1–7. This proposal deliberately leaves the relay module model and source/sink selection conditional; no 24 V terminal is directly connected to Smoothie. Keep NOT-Aus/guards/contactor chain hardwired and independent. The 2.2 kW spindle power path uses correct nameplate mains/filter to VFD input and WJ200 U/T1, V/T2, W/T3 plus PE to spindle; no mains contacts are guessed here. See the separate fully named ULN2003AN/AQY212GS process-run proposal; no generic unspecified relay is needed.
  13. Machine limits, references and NOT-Aus relay · source functions known, wire endpoints incomplete: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. The plan depicts three references, six end switches, independent NOT-Aus and a relay with a sensing contact plus a separate 12 V coil chain. No pin/cavity contacts or NO/NC states are assigned. Keep OPEN until each wire and return is traced; never route the E-stop coil through a Smoothie output.
  14. X axis · six manufacturer switch functions to candidate SmoothieBox inputs: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Functional proposal only: J24–J29 pin3 are mapped GPIO inputs with pin2 GND and pin1 selector/contact purpose to confirm. J36/J37/J38 pin3 are exposed GPIOs; pin10 is GND. The machine plan shows one X/Y/Z reference plus min/max end switches, but does not map specific wire/cabinet contacts. Therefore every proposed signal and return is dotted; identify each physical switch wire, state and interface before wiring.
  15. Y axis · six manufacturer switch functions to candidate SmoothieBox inputs: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Functional proposal only: J24–J29 pin3 are mapped GPIO inputs with pin2 GND and pin1 selector/contact purpose to confirm. J36/J37/J38 pin3 are exposed GPIOs; pin10 is GND. The machine plan shows one X/Y/Z reference plus min/max end switches, but does not map specific wire/cabinet contacts. Therefore every proposed signal and return is dotted; identify each physical switch wire, state and interface before wiring.
  16. Z axis · six manufacturer switch functions to candidate SmoothieBox inputs: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Functional proposal only: J24–J29 pin3 are mapped GPIO inputs with pin2 GND and pin1 selector/contact purpose to confirm. J36/J37/J38 pin3 are exposed GPIOs; pin10 is GND. The machine plan shows one X/Y/Z reference plus min/max end switches, but does not map specific wire/cabinet contacts. Therefore every proposed signal and return is dotted; identify each physical switch wire, state and interface before wiring.
  17. Isolated run LED drive · power, bias and every IC pin: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.
  18. Isolated run contact · machine side only: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. These contact pins belong to the same U6 used in the preceding LED-drive panel. The optically isolated contact side has no conductor to SmoothieBox or ULN logic GND. Retain independent hardware stop/safety. Verify receiver mode and off-state leakage; this is a new unverified process-run proposal.

New regulated +5V opto rail is separate from motor power; tie its logic return deliberately to buffer and case signal references. ENA is an optional commissioning-gated drive control, not E-stop/STO. Leave ENA pair disconnected if fitted-drive manual/default-enable strategy requires it; then also leave corresponding case ENABLE/ENABLE GND unused. No motor winding connects to a case DRV signal. Pin correction: TI SN74LVC07A Rev W, page 4, assigns 4A input to pin 9 and 4Y output to pin 8; tie unused input pin 9 to the documented reference, leave unused output pin 8 unconnected.

Hitachi WJ200 · SmoothieBox PWM to O-L analog speed referenceTI OPA197 · U3A + passive gain stage · pin 1 NC · leave unconnectedNo conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Specific electronic proposal: R1=10 kΩ, C1=10 µF, OPA197 U3 SOIC-8: pin6 OUT, pin7 V+, pin4 V−, pin3 +IN, pin2 −IN; pins1/5/8 NC, Rf=10 kΩ and Rg=10 kΩ yield nominal gain 2.0 (verified 0–5 V case PWM average -> 0–10 V). It is not isolated, is not installed hardware, and requires scope/ground-noise/transfer validation. No run/stop or safety function is provided. OPA197 SOIC-8 NC pins 1, 5 and 8 are not connected. Fit local 100 nF bypass between U3 pins7 and4, plus nearby bulk capacitance chosen for supply impedance. R1/C1 time constant is 0.1 s: verify ripple and speed response at chosen PWM frequency. Sequence supplies so GPIO does not drive an unpowered stage; prove zero-command/startup output and loss-of-command shutdown. The case reference proposes 5V PWM rather than direct 3V3 GPIO: this selected converter uses gain 2. Verify actual output amplitude/polarity, rail tolerance, clipping and transfer with VFD disconnected; do not use the earlier gain-3 Core circuit on a 5V case signal.

Isolated run LED drive · power, bias and every IC pinU5 · TI ULN2003AN · DIP16 · 9 · COM · NC (noninductive LED)No conductor drawn. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.

Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.

Earlier alternative retrofit architecture and circuit details

Owner retrofit guide · DeineFraesmaschine CNC mill

The manufacturer electrical plan 012-001-001-01 Rev A (2020-09-01) documents four DQ860 drivers, a WJ200 and switch/relay chains, but is not an as-built revision record. This diagram shows a concrete buffered STEP/DIR proposal with the exact SN74LVC07A pins and +5 V common-anode endpoints. Axis pairing and buffer-to-machine references are dotted pending continuity/nameplate checks. The WJ200 input circuit is shown as a discrete 0–10 V converter proposal and configured run input; the machine safety relay remains independent.

Which Smoothie target fits this machine?
  • SmoothieBox: SmoothieBox enclosure plus V2 Core is the supported direction when retaining the four DQ860 power stages. The housing/carrier must expose verified Core P1 STEP/DIR and ground contacts.
  • V2 Core: Recommended controller for this machine: Core provides four STEP/DIR channels; add two TI SN74LVC07A open-drain buffers only if measured driver input current, pull-up, supply reference and return meet the device datasheet. Driver signal common to −36 V relationship is not documented.
  • V2 Prime: Not recommended for the documented 36 V/large external-driver architecture as a replacement power stage. Prime motor outputs are winding power, not STEP/DIR; a Core-compatible STEP/DIR peripheral would be required to keep DQ860s.
Retain or add
  • Two TI SN74LVC07A open-drain six-channel buffer ICs; verify guaranteed sink current and exact DQ860 input current before wiring.
  • Retain four DQ860-family drives, four motors, 36 V/10 A supply, 12 V/10 A supply, WJ200, filter and physical NOT-Aus/relay chain pending nameplate/current verification.
  • OPA197 U3 SOIC-8 analog stage parts (R1 10kΩ, C1 10µF, Rf 20kΩ, Rg 10kΩ) are an unisolated engineering proposal; alternatively select a documented isolated PWM-to-0–10 V product matching configured PWM.
  • A separately selected opto-relay/dry-contact interface for the WJ200 FW input; no direct 3.3 V GPIO into P24/PLC input circuit.
  • Proposed run stage: TI ULN2003AN DIP16, Panasonic AQY212GS SOP4, 390 Ω LED resistor and 10 kΩ GPIO pull-down; separate PCB isolation layout required. Slow process command only; never substitute for safety chain.
Conversion and commissioning
  1. Verify exact plan revision against fitted cabinet, every DQ860 suffix/current/DIP setting, WJ200 model and spindle nameplate.
  2. Trace each motor to its driver and identify winding pairs by continuity with power isolated; keep +36 V/GND power wiring on driver only.
  3. Build/review U1 and U2 SN74LVC07A buffers with VCC pin14=3.3 V, GND pin7=the individually identified Core J5.19 reference, and exact input/output pins shown in the diagram. Compare DQ input current to TI guaranteed output-low test point; if unknown or over spec, choose a rated isolated sink module.
  4. For WJ200 analog speed, verify 0–10V O-L input, exact analog return, selected voltage input/source parameters, output range and grounding; run must be separate configured FW contact.
  5. Identify all nine switch contacts and relay contacts individually; prove hardwired E-stop and guard behavior independently before motion commissioning.

Pin-by-pin schedule and commissioning notes
  • DQ driver block functions per manufacturer plan: PUL+, PUL−, DIR+, DIR−, ENBL+, ENBL−, VDC/+36 V, GND/−36 V, A+, A−, B+, B−. Four blocks, axis mapping not documented. Machine plan shows +5 V references but no complete numbered signal-return topology.
  • SN74LVC07A exact SOIC-14 mapping (TI datasheet Rev W): pin1 1A,2 1Y;3 2A,4 2Y;5 3A,6 3Y;7 GND;8 4Y,9 4A;10 5Y,11 5A;12 6Y,13 6A;14 VCC. Open-drain output pin voltage max 5.5 V; check sink current and actual DQ input current before use.
  • WJ200 terminal schedule top SN,7,6,5,4,3,2,1,L,PLC,P24; bottom SP,EO,EA,H,O,OI,L,AM,CM2,12,11. O-L voltage input 0–10 V; terminal 1 must be parameterized as FW; P24/PLC logic source/sink mode must match chosen isolated contact circuit.
  • Core P1 J5 STEP/DIR: .16/.18, .22/.24, .28/.30, .34/.36; shared enable .13. Ground contacts .19,.20,.25,.26,.32,.37,.38,.39; J35.6 PWM, .7 TTL candidate, .10 GND. Core input candidates: J24–J29 pin3 signal/pin2 GND/pin1 selector; candidate reference GPIO J36/J37/J38 pin3 and GND pin10. Slash is net namespace, not logic inversion.
  • Plan has 3 reference switches, 6 end switches, separate NOT-Aus switch and relay P10/GND sense plus distinct +12 V coil chain; source does not enumerate switch NO/NC, controller pins or safety performance. PC_SUB-D shell/cavities and breakout assignment unknown; do not invent DB25 contacts.

Retained first-pass wiki-only dossier and sources · historical scope

Complete wiki-researched machine dossier

DeineFraesmaschine

Evidence depth: gantry CNC mill; open-source-inspired catalogue entry. This dossier is grounded in the Appropedia wiki entry only; references from that page to vendor, GitHub, or other non-wiki pages were not used as evidence.

Wiki-supported identity and facts

The wiki catalogue lists 350 × 380 × 160 mm, a water-cooled spindle, and Mach3 software. It describes the construction as well documented, but this research pass does not use the linked non-wiki documentation.

Use, visuals, and electrical connections

The captured wiki catalogue row does not provide a machine-specific operating procedure, connector table, electrical pinout, or transcribable wiring diagram for this model. Those items are recorded as unknown, rather than inferred from the model name or from the page's external links. The source may link further documentation, but that non-wiki content is outside this source-only research scope.

Source

  • Tolocar / Open Source Machine Tools — Appropedia wiki — machine catalogue entry.

Wiki sources

Sources are restricted to the supplied wiki-research dossier. External vendor/forum links are not used as evidence.

  1. Tolocar / Open Source Machine Tools — Appropedia wiki

Hitachi WJ200 NT337X · conditional input and PE reference

For a matching NT337X, the April 2013 manual’s printed 2-18 (PDF page 66) describes single-phase input on R/L1 and T/L3, with S/L2 unused. The selected drawing labels the physical pair L1/N, so match the fitted terminal-face markings instead of relabelling terminals. Its three-phase drawing uses R/L1, S/L2 and T/L3. Motor outputs are U/T1, V/T2 and W/T3; protective earth remains separate from signal grounds. Do not extrapolate the anomalous printed 2-19 diagrams.

This is an exclusive, model-qualified manufacturer reference, not evidence of the installed drive or its wiring. Check the drive nameplate and motor supply class, preserve the existing mains protection and independent stop, and verify the protective-earth connection. The current main includes the canonical conditional interface circuits; the complete manufacturer VFD alternatives remain supplementary references. Drive identity, mains supply class, protective earth and fitted wiring remain unqualified.

Sources: captured local manual · April 2013, PDF p. 66; HiFlex mirror of the manufacturer-authored manual · PDF p. 66.

Retained research chapter · wiki-134-dotted-mach3-stepdir-example-2026-09-27
DeineFraesmaschine · dotted Mach3 STEP/DIR function hypotheses · 2026-09-27 — six user-requested guesses into a hypothetical DB25 peripheral; all other machine paths remain OPEN.

The manufacturer plan identifies Mach3 and page 5's PC_SUB-D connection but provides no PC-to-breakout cavity map or confirmed 25-contact housing. The official Mach3Mill Setup tutorial gives a three-axis example: X STEP/DIR on parallel-port pins 2/3, Y on 4/5 and Z on 6/7. It explicitly says actual machine pinouts may differ. Those six function relationships now appear as dotted violet GUESS lines from SmoothieBox X/Y/Z STEP/DIR contacts to conditional contacts 2–7 on the peripheral card. This follows the requested peripheral layout; it is not an installed pin map or a qualified connection.

Reference: Mach3Mill Setup tutorial release 2.5, pp. 2–3. The tutorial warns that the actual machine pinout may differ. The manufacturer plan and tutorial do not qualify the fitted shell, breakout map, SmoothieBox drive capability, levels, current, timing, polarity, isolation, signal reference, installed cable or safety behavior. The dotted lines are research hypotheses; do not wire from them.

The regenerated SVGs now use the SmoothieBox source diagram's field-connector language: a clean exterior perimeter, grouped connector shells, explicit INTERNAL BOARD, DRIVER CIRCUIT AND SAFETY LOGIC OMITTED scope, readable outside-contact labels, and machine-side peripheral cards with evidence-colored accents. Profile figures remain column-width images and use the existing native-dialog lightbox: first click opens the full image, second click enters pan/zoom mode, and Escape closes. A structural audit found 231 legacy profile disclosures, all closed by default; earlier diagrams remain retained.
